# V17 paired voice comparison — part 2/3

These accepted V17 supporting registers serve the existing Humor/Voice ledger responsibilities as volume evidence appendices. Publication and whole-tranche adjudication remain separate. Original discovery, assignments and authored-time statements remain exact; current errata are explicitly external. Same-model conditional independence and receiving limits are recorded in the [V17 receiving review](../09%20Audits%20and%20Manifests/KONOSUBA_V17_RECEIVING_REVIEW.md). Exact source members are text/part0009.html (C1), part0010.html (C2), part0011.html (C3), part0012.html (F), part0013.html (Ep); excluded bodies/images/holdouts remain unopened in this continuation.

Fixed original-order records 82–162, 81 records. Order is P99 then I144, not selected by result. [Navigation/scope/exact-text catalog](KONOSUBA_V17_VOICE_COMPARISON_REGISTER.md) governs all V references. Each table value is compact JSON with one reversible HTML escape for &,<,>,|; JSON carries literal newline/backslash/quote escapes. {"@exact_text":"ID"} expands to the exact string in the named catalog. This is lossless factoring of repeated text, not a shortened rationale. Every unique initial rationale is printed in full, in its own labeled row; its value is factored out of the otherwise exact initial field bundle and restored by the inverse proof. Proof parses actual Markdown rows, reverses escapes/references, and compares every projected field to the immutable initial inputs. Private full novel bases/windows, ruby XML/pairs and long selected quotations are omitted by the declared projection; their hashes/locators and initial analytical metadata remain. No global spacing/reflow is applied to original strings. Native literal joins verify text and location, not by themselves speaker, addressee, literal-gloss accuracy or pragmatic judgment; those remain substantive receiving responsibilities.

### IV17-038

| Field | Exact value / declared projection |
|---|---|
| id | "IV17-038" |
| fixed_discovery_lineage | "independent" |
| producer_initial_disposition_exact | "PARTIAL" |
| producer_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Dangerous escort137–140 independently sustains an already active escape task. After private rebuke145,148 asks Aqua to endure and explains the numerical danger, a concrete coordination instruction rather than merely performed enemy swagger. Before119 already operationally manipulates the guards, however, and changes from public Noss impersonation to private ally address make a clean stylistic onset unidentifiable. This is limited continuity under intensified danger, not a new caring personality." |
| independent_initial_role_phase_exact | "ACTIVE_TACTICAL_CARE" |
| independent_initial_speech_adequacy_exact | "ADEQUATE" |
| independent_initial_function_direction_exact | "MIXED_AUDIENCE_CONFOUND" |
| independent_full_initial_rationale_exact | "Kazuma already offers a strategic hostage plan in his performed Noss persona.
